1a1(rgyud): @#//sangs rgyas mnyam sbyor kyi brgyud bzhugs s+ho//
1b1(rgyud): @#//rgyal ba ras dang /
2a1(rgyud): @#/ O /sarba bud d+ha sa ma yo ga/DA ki ni mA yA/sham 
2a2(rgyud): b+ha ra na ma/ud ta ra tan tra/bod skad du/sangs rgyas tham+sa 
2a3(rgyud): cad dang mnyam par sbyor ba/mkha' 'gro ma sgyu ma/bde ba'i 
2b1(rgyud): mchog ces bya ba'i rgyud phyi ma/dpal rdo rje sems dpa' la phyag 'tshal lo//
2b2(rgyud): sems dpa' sangs rgyas kun gyi dngos//rdo rje sems dpa' bde ba'i mchog/gsang 
2b3(rgyud): ba mchog gi dges pa na//thams cad bdag nyid rtag tu bzhugs/'di ni rang 
2b4(rgyud): 'byung bcom ldan 'das/gcig tu rab tu che ba'i lha//sangs rgyas thams cad 
3a1(rgyud): @#/7/mnyam sbyor ba/mkha' 'gro sgyu ma bde ba'i mchog/chags med chags 
3a2(rgyud): dang bral ba min//dbu mar yang ni mi dmigs so//bud med kun gyi rgyu ma'i rgya//'di 
3a3(rgyud): ni gnyis med theg pa'i mchog/sgyu ma dag ni thams cad pas//bud med sgyu ma khyad 
3a4(rgyud): par che//ngo bo nyid kyis mthu dag gis//rang bzhin gyis ni de grub pa'i//'jig 
3b1(rgyud): rten gsum 'dir bud med 'di//gus par byed pa dag tu 'gyur//brnyed dang bde dang dga' kun 
3b2(rgyud): gyis//ngan pa spyod pa 'ang 'grub par 'gyur//rang gi ngo bo yongs bsgyur nas//bud med kun gyis 
3b3(rgyud): sgyu ma 'grub//ngo mtshar sgyu ma'i phyag rgya 'di//mkha' 'gro ma zhes brtag bstan te/de ni namakha' 'gro 
3b4(rgyud): ba zhes//khams las mngon du brtags pa ste//nam mkha' kun du spyod 'grub pas//mkha' 
3b5(rgyud): 'gro ma zhes rab tu grags//thams cad ma lus bde mchog gis//sangs rgyas thams cad 
4a1(rgyud): @#/ O /mnyam sbyor ba//mkha' 'gro ma zhes rab du grags//de ni namakha' 'gro ba zhes/sangs 
4a2(rgyud): rgyas khams las rnam par brtags//kun du 'gro ba 'grub pa ni//sangs rgyas kun bdag mkha' 'gro 
4a3(rgyud): ma'o//ma zhes bya ba bde bar bshad//sangs rgyas kun gyi bde chen yin//sgyu ma thams cad rab sbyor ba/
4a4(rgyud): mchog tu bde bas bde ba'i mchog/kun tu kun nas thams cad ni//bdag nyid rtag tu thams cad kun//
4a5(rgyud): sangs rgyas la sogs rtan g.yo kun //kun gyis dngos por bdag gyur to//sems dang sems dpa'i 
4b1(rgyud): ting 'dzin kun//byang chub spyod pa sna tshogs nyid//de bzhin bdud dang bdud las rgyal//de ni byang chub sangs rgyas 
4b2(rgyud): yin//rdo rje rdo rje 'dzin nyid dang //de bzhin pad ma (pad ma )'chang //nor bu nor bu 'dzin nyid dang //de dag gi yang 
4b3(rgyud): rigs yin no//de nyid 'dod chags 'dod chags bral//'dod pa dang ni grol ba 'ang yin//dus gsum srid 
4b4(rgyud): gsum mchog gsum dang //'jig rten gsum dang khams gsum dang //rnam pa sna tshogs tha dad pa'i//
4b5(rgyud): gnas pa thams cad de nyid do//'gro ba dag ni ma lus kun//thams cad du ni 'di 'gyur ro//de la sogs pa 
4b6(rgyud): mtha' yas mchog/chos dbyings mnyam dang mi mnyam pa//nam mkha'i dbyings kyi mtha' klas pa'i//dngos 
5a1(rgyud): @#/ O /po thams cad de yin te/nam mkha' kun gyi skabs na dpal//rdo rje sems dpa' de 
5a2(rgyud): bzhin gshegs//sangs rgyas thams cad mnyam sbyor ba//mkha' 'gro sgyu ma bde mchog go/bskal pa 
5a3(rgyud): bye ba grangs med par//sangs rgyas thams cad thob pa yi//phyag rgya'i cho ga gang dag gis//tshe 'di 
5a4(rgyud): nyid kyis thob 'gyur ba//de ni bdag gis thob par bya//sangs rgyas kun bdag bde ba'i mchog/
5a5(rgyud): bdag nyid shin tu bsgrub pa la//sangs rgyas kun bdag bde ba'i mchog /rnal 'byor yang dag ma yin 
5a6(rgyud): sems//lha la dmigs par byed pa dag /rnal 'byor gzugs brnyan rang bzhin te//lugs ma 
5b1(rgyud): la sogs rnal 'byor skye//yang dag mchog sems rnal 'byor pa//lha la dmigs par byed pa dag/rang gi dam tshig 
5b2(rgyud): bdag sbyor ba//mi 'gyur dam pa 'grub par 'gyur/gzugs brnyan lugs ma la sogs las//rnal 'byor 'byung 
5b3(rgyud): bar mi 'gyur gyi//byang chub sems la rab brtson na//de las rnal 'byor lhar 'gyuro//byang chub sems ni rdo rje ste//sangs rgyas 
5b4(rgyud): kun gyi bdag nyid yin//de bas thams cad bdag sbyor bas//sangs rgyas thams cad thob par 'gyur//bdag nyid sangs rgyas thams cad 
5b5(rgyud): dang //dpa' bo thams cad bdag yin no//de bas bdag nyid lhar sbyor bas//bdag nyid rab tu bsgrub par bya//de yis sangs rgyas thams cad
5b6(rgyud): dang //dpa' bo rnal 'byor thams cad dang //rdo rje 'dzin pa thams cad ni//tshe 'di nyid la 'grub par 'gyur//bcom ldan 'das dpal rdo rje sems 
5b7(rgyud): dpas de skad gsungs so//sangs rgyas thams cad dang mnyam par sbyor ba//mkha' 
6a1(rgyud): @#/ O /'gro sgyu ma bde ba'i mchog las /de kho na nyid snang ba dam pa'i yul la 'jug pa ye shes kyis phyag (rgya)brtag pa ste //'di ni rnal 'byor 
6a2(rgyud): bcom ldan 'das /rdo rje sems dpa' de bzhin gshegs//sangs rgyas tham+sa cad mnyam par sbyor//mkha' 'gro sgyu ma bde ba'i mchog/rdo rje sems dpa' (bdag)sbyor bas/
6a3(rgyud): dbab pa thams cad rab tu 'grub//sangs rgyas (thab+sa)cad mnyam sbyor ba//rnal 'byor dbang phyug bde ba che//de bas bde ba nyid kyis ni//sangs rgyas bde
6a4(rgyud): ba kun 'thob pas//sangs rgyas thams cad mnyam sbyor ba//rnala'abyor dbang phyug bde chen po//'di la khrus dang bsdam mi dgos//dka' thub
6a5(rgyud): dka' ba spyad mi dgos//dka' thub sdom pa med pa dam//bde ba dang ni dga' bas 'grub//de las ji ltar 'dod spyod cing//kun za de bzhin 
6a6(rgyud): thams cad spyod//spyod pa ci dga' 'dod par byed//g.yo ba dga' kyang ci dgar byed//langs pa dang ni 'dug pa dang//'chag gam ji ltar gnas 
6a7(rgyud): kyang rung//dgod dam rab tu smra yang rung //gang na ji ltar spyod kyang rung//dkyil 'khor du yang ma zhugs pa 'am//sgrib pa kun dang ldan yang rung//rang
6a8(rgyud): gi lhar ni bdag sbyar ba//bsod nams chung yang 'grub par 'gyur//de nyid rnal 'byor 'di yis ni//thams cad sgrub pa byed pa na//ngan zos ngan spyod tham+sa
6b1(rgyud): cad kyis//nyes pa dag tu yong mi 'gyur//bskal pa bye bar mi thob pa//sangs rgyas dam pa thams cad de//phyag rgya'i cho ga gang dag gis//bsgrub na kun du thob 'gyur ba/
6b2(rgyud): bdag nyid sgrub par byed pa'i mchog/de ni bdag nyid brtag par bya//sangs rgyas thams cad mnyam sbyor ba//mkha' 'gro sgyu ma bde ba'i mchog/rdo rje sems dpa' bdag
6b3(rgyud): sbyor ba//cha lugs ji bzhin bsgyur ba yis//thams cad sna tshogs phyag rgya'i mchog//bsod nams chung yang 'grub par 'gyur//rgyud 'di sgrubs pa tsam gyis ni//sangs
6b4(rgyud): rgyas thams cad byin gyis brlob//shin tu byin gyis brlabs na ni//sangs rgyas thams cad 'du bar 'gyur//sangs rgyas kun dang mnyam sbyor bas//sangs rgyas kun dang ma nyam par 'gyur/
6b5(rgyud): sangs rgyas kun dang mnyam pa'i phyir//sangs rgyas thams cad grub par 'gyur//gang gis ldan pa 'di mthong zhing //rnaM pa kun tu mchod byed na//de yis sangs rgyas thaMd kyang //mthong zhing mchod 
6b6(rgyud): pa byas par 'gyur//'di dang sangs rgyas thams cad la//mthong ba dang ni rig pa yis//dkyil 'khor bdag tu bzhugs pas//bden pa dag kyang mthong bar 'gyur//mthong ba dang ni reg
6b7(rgyud): pa dang //thos pa dang ni drang pa yis//sdig pa kun las rnam grol zhing //sangs rgyas dngos grub thams cad 'grub//'di dang sangs rgyas thabs cad la//rnal 'byor thams cad mnyam sbyor ba/
6b8(rgyud): bud med dag kyang rnam grol zhing //sangs sargyas byang chub reg par 'gyur//kun tu kun nas thams cad ni//bdag nyid rtag par thams cad du//sangs rgyas thams cad dngos
7a1(rgyud): mnyam sbyor @#/ O /grub par !/de yis bdag nyid mthong bar 'gyur//'di ni bdag nyid kun gnas pas//mchod pa'i sprin chen thams cad kyis/bkod 
7a2(rgyud): pa rab 'byam tshogs dag gis//de bzhin gshegs pa mchod pa mdzes//thams cad bdag nyid sbyor mchod dang //rdo rje sems dpa' mchog nyid ni//sangs rgyas kun 
7a3(rgyud): gyi rang bzhin te//bde ba'i mchog (na)'grub par 'gyur//rnal 'byor thams cad nyer sbyor bas;;dpal ni gzhir gyur 'grub par 'gyur//rnal 'byor kun ni bcom 
7a4(rgyud): ldan 'das//rdo rje sems dpa' de bzhin gshegs//de yi nye bar sbyor ba ni//khams gsum ma lus thams cad do//rnal 'byor kun gyis longs spyod kyis//
7a5(rgyud): ji ltar bde bar bsnyen byed cing //rang gi lha yi rnal 'byor gyis//bdag nyid rab tu mchod par bya//rjes su sbyor bas mchod byed byed//rnal 'byor kun tu bde ba 
7a6(rgyud): dag/bdag nyid kun du myang byed na//shin tu sbyor bas 'grub par 'gyur//sangs rgyas kun gyi bdag nyid kyis//rang sa ye na bde ba 'dis//rdo rje sems dpa' 
7a7(rgyud): dpal tshe dang //lang 'tsho nad med bde mchog 'grub/kun du kun nas thams cad du//sangs rgyas thams cad mnyam sbyor ba//mkha' 'gro sgyu ma bde ba'i mchog/
7b1(rgyud): rtag tu de ni 'grub par 'gyur//bcom ldan 'das dpal rdo rje sems dpas de skad gsungs so//sangs rgyas thams cad dang mnyam par sbyor ba mkha' 'gro sgyu ma bde ba'i 
7b2(rgyud): mchog las//dpal rdo rje sems dpa' kun du sbyor ba'i brtag pa ste gnyis pa'o////'di ni rnal 'byor bcom ldan 'das/rdo rje sems dpa' de bzhin gshegs//
7b3(rgyud): sangs rgyas thams cad mnyam sbyor bas//mkha' 'gro sgyu ma bde mchog dpal//rdo rje sems dpa'i rnal 'byor ba//dpal ldan rang gi daM tshig ste//sangs rgyas thams cad 
7b4(rgyud): mnyam sbyor ba'i//rnal 'byor dbang phyug bde chen dpal//rdo rje sems dpa' sbyor ba yi//mthu nyid kyis ni thams cad du//phyag rgya bde ba chen po kun//so sor rjes 
7b5(rgyud): su rab 'grub 'gyur//dpal ldan rdo rje sems sbyor ba'i//mthu nyid kyis ni thamasacad du//rnal 'byor bde ba mos pa kun//so sor rjes su rab 'grub 'gyur//dpa' 
7b6(rgyud): ldan rdo rje sems sbyor ba'i//mthu nyid kyis ni thams can du//bde ba mos pa'i dbang phyug kun//so sor rjes su rang 'grub 'gyur//dpal ldanardo rje sems sbyor ba'i//
7b7(rgyud): mthu nyid kyis ni thams cang du//namakha'i dbyings kyi mtha' klas pa'i//bud med mchog rnams skye bar 'gyur//de yi dam tshig des 'byung ba//sdug pa dam tshig bdag 
8a1(rgyud): mnyam sbyor @#/ O /rjes 'brang //shin tu sbyor dang sbyor chen po//thams cad bdag nyid dam tshig mchog/gcig bzhin du ni cig spyod pa//lhan cig spyod 
8a2(rgyud): pa rjes spyad sdug/sangs rgyas thams cad mnyam sbyor ba//mkha' 'gro sgyu ma mchog tu 'bar //thams cad dga' ba ma lus pa'i//phyag rgya thams cad bde ba che/
8a3(rgyud): rtag pa dam pa lha mo yi//rgyal mos mchod pa thams cad mchog/dpal ldan rdo rje sems dpa' sbyor//thams cad lha mo 'dus pas ni//ma lus bskal pa bzang 
8a4(rgyud): po kun//bde bar so sor rjes su 'grub//phyag rgya 'di yis btab pa las//rdo rje sems dpa' che ba lha//namakha'i dbyings kyis mtha' klas pa/lha mo bde ba chen 
8a5(rgyud): po kun //thams cad rnal 'byor bde bas myong //sdig med stobs kyis ngo mthon du/phan tshun du ni rab dga' bas//skyes bu'i tshul gyis dga' bar byed//dbang po 
8a6(rgyud): kun las byung ba yi//mchod pa'i sprin chen thams cad kyi//bkod pa rab 'byam sna tshogs kyis//thams cad bdag nyid mchod byed byed//rnal 'byor bde ba thams cad kyi/
8a7(rgyud): dbang bskur bde chen las byung ba //rin chen sna tshogs thams cad kyis//rab tu smos pas dbang bskur 'gyur//lha mo kun gyi longs spyod la//ci bde bar ni bsnyen bya ba//
8b1(rgyud): bdag ni che ba'i lhar sbyor bas//bdag la bdag gis rab tu mchod//rnal 'byor thams cad bde ba yis//rjes su sbyor pas mchod byed cing //rtag tu myang bar byed pa na/
8b2(rgyud): shin tu sbyor bas rab 'grub 'gyur//sangs rgyas thams cad bdag nyid kyi//ra sa ya na'i bde des dpal//rdo rje sems dpa' tshe 'grub cing //lang tsho nad med bde mchog 
8b3(rgyud): 'grub|sangs rgyas kun gyi sku chen dang //sangs rgyas kun gyi gsung snyan pa//sangs rgyas kun gyi thugs che ba//sangs rgyas kun gyi mchod pa che//sangs rgyas kun gyi rgyal chen 
8b4(rgyud): po//rdo rje 'dzin pa kun gyi bdag/'jig rten dbang phyug kun gyi gtso//rin chen dbang phyug kun gyi bdag/de dag dang ni dga' spyod na//'dod pa bzhin du 'gro 'ong lding //lha 
8b5(rgyud): mo thams cad grub chen po//'khor los bsgyur par rab tu 'gyur//chos dbyings mnyam dang mi mnyam pa//namakha'i dbyings kyi mtha' klas pas//dngos grub kun gyi bde 
8b6(rgyud): mos pa//de las rgya che mchog gzhan med//sangs rgyas thams cad mnyam sbyor ba//rnal 'byor dbang phyug chen po des//'di la tshe 'di nyid kyis ni//rnam pa kun tu mi 
8b7(rgyud): 'grub pa'i//dngos grub kun gyi bdemos pa//chos dbyings mnyam dngami mnyam pa//namakha'i dbyangs kyi mtha' klas par//dbang phyug kun gyi bde mos pa//de las 
9a1(rgyud): mnyam sbyor @#/ O /cha chen gang yang med //sangs rgyas thams cad mnyam sbyor ba//mkha' 'gro sgyu ma bde mchog des//'di la tshe 'di nyid kyis ni//rnam pa kun du  
9a2(rgyud): mi 'grub med//bcom ldan 'das dpal rdo rjes sems dpas de skad gsungs so//sangs rgyas thams cad (   )mnyam par sbyor ba//mkha' 'gro ma sgyu ma bde ba'i mchog 
9a3(rgyud): las//lha mo thams cad dang mnyam par sbyor ba bskal pa bzang po dpal rdo rje dam tshig gi brtag pa ste gsum pa'o////'di ni rnal 'byor bcom ldan 'das//rdo rje 
9a4(rgyud): sems dpa' de bzhin gshegs//sangs rgyas thams cad la mnyam sbyor ba//mkha' 'gro sgyu ma bde ba'i mchog/lha mo kun dang mnyam sbyor ba//bskal pa bzang mchog 
9a5(rgyud): 'dod pa ste//yang dag sbyor ba'i bde myong ba//'dod chags ther zug brtan pa can//sangs rgyas kun gyi gzugs chen dang //dbyangs dang dri dang ro dag dang //sangs 
9a6(rgyud): rgyas kun gyi reg bde mchog/rang gi spyad par de spyod do//sangs rgyas pha rol phyin grub pa//gzungs dang sa rnams de bzhin du//dga' bar spyod na lha dang mi//bskal 
9a7(rgyud): bzang bud med ci zhig smos//lha'i dngos grub dga' ba dang //sangs rgyas byang chub bde chen po//bskal pa bzang po bde myong bas//so so rjes su rab 'grub 'gyur//
9b1(rgyud): dngos grub thams cad las byung ba//de bzhin gshegs pa kun gyi dpal//bskal pa bzang po longs spyod kun//rang gi spyod par de spyod do//rdo rje can gyi grub mchog dang //
9b2(rgyud): 'jig rten dbang po mchog gi dpal//sangs rgyas kun las bde dam pa//phrogs nas nye bar spyod par byed//khams gsum dag na ma lus pa'i//sangs rgyas rgyal srid
9b3(rgyud): chen po kun//rtsal gyi stobs kyis ngos 'thon du//phrogs nas nye bar spyad par byed//sangs rgyas kun gyi grub chen po//mchog gi dbang phyug dam pa dag/kun nas phrogs
9b4(rgyud): te de spyod na//longs spyod gzhan lta smos ci dgos//mthu chen las ni bkra shis brtan//phrogs las grub dang dran las dga'//ngo 'thon stobs kyis phrogs nas kyang //
9b5(rgyud): de ni dad la sbyin par byed//ma lus 'jig rten gsum rgyal srid//'jig rten dbang phyug kun dang bcas//ngo mthon stobs kyis phrogs nasakyang //de ni dad pas sbyin par
9b6(rgyud): byed//tshangs pa 'jug sel dbang chen gyi//mchog gi dbang phyug  dam pa dag/kun nas phogs te myur sbyin na//longs spyod gzhan la ci zhig smos//de yis mi sbyin ci
9b7(rgyud): yang med//sangs rgyas don du bya ba kun//mi bya ba ni gang yang med//sbyin pa dang ni byed pa'ang yin//sangs rgyas byang chub sems dpa' yang //phrogs nas kyang ni sbyin byed
10a1(rgyud): @#/ O /na|las mchog dag ni rab 'byam dang //dngos grub gzhan lta ci zhig smos//dngos grub mchog ni gdon mi za//sde rnams thams cad rab 'joms pa//
10a2(rgyud): sangs rgyas kun gyi grub chen po//'khor los bsgyur bar rab 'grub pa//sangs rgyas thams cad mnyam sbyor ba//mkha' 'gro sgyu ma bde mchog gis//sangs rgyas kun gyi rdzu 
10a3(rgyud): 'phrul che//rnam par 'phrul pa rab tu 'jug/la lar byang chub sems chen po//la lar spyod pa 'thun par 'gro/la lar dga' ldan lha dag las//dam pa shin tu 'phro ba yi//
10a4(rgyud): la lar skye ba rnam par dag/la lar nges par 'byung ba gsal//la lar byang chub chen por gshegs//la lar bdud las rab tu rgyal//la lar byang chub mngon 'tshang rgya//
10a5(rgyud): la lar chos kyi 'khor lo bskor//la lar pha rol mu stegs rnams//chos dang 'thun par tshar bcad do//la lar 'jig rten gsum rgyal ba//grub pa kun gyi dbang phyug nyid//
10a6(rgyud): la lar rtog pa thams cad mchog/grub (pa'i)dbang phyug bla na med//de lasogs pa mtha' yas mchog/sangs rgyas kun bdag bde mchog gi/'dul ba'i thabs su nges pa'i don//
10a7(rgyud): rnam par 'phrul pas dges shing rol//dngos grub chen po kun gyi mchog/brtan pa dbang phyug che ba'i bdag /gzugs dang tshe dang kha dog stobs//dpal ldan rdo rje 
10b1(rgyud): sems dpa' bzhin/'gro ba bdag gis bsnyen bya zhing //de bzhin gshegs pa kun gyis mchod//lha mo kun gyis dpal ldan mchog/rdo rje sems dpar rab tu 
10b2(rgyud): 'grub//sems can khams ni du ma dag/ma lus par ni phyag rgyas 'debs//lha dang byang chub sems dga' dang //brtan par sems can rnams la sbyin//sangs rgyas 
10b3(rgyud): kun bdag bde mchog gis/sems can kun gyi don byed cing //ma lus lus pa med pa yi//bskal pa kun tu srid par gnas//bcom ldan 'das dpal rdo rje sems dpas 
10b4(rgyud): de skad gsungs so//sangs rgyas thams cad dang mnyam par sbyor ba mkha' 'gro sgyu ma bde ba'i mchog las//sna tshogs thams cad kyi bskal pa bzang po mchog gi dngos 
10b5(rgyud): grub ces bya ba//dpal dam tshig gi brtag pa ste bzhi pa'o////'di ni rnal 'byor bcom ldan 'das/rdo rje sems dpa' de bzhin gshegs//sangs rgyas thams 
10b6(rgyud): cad mnyam sbyor ba//mkha' 'gro sgyu ma bde ba'i chog/yang dag bsgrub pa gcig bu nyid//ji ltar 'dod pa bzhin du sbyar//rtog pa'i go cha thams cad la//grub pa 
10b7(rgyud): dam pa mchog gi mchog/sna tshogs las kyi sbyor ba yis//cho ga sna tshogs 'dod pa yis//sems can rnams ni 'dul ba dang //bde ba thams cad 'grub pa'i phyir//
11a1(rgyud): mnyam sbyor @#/ O /bcom ldan rnal 'byor de nyid ni//sangs rgyas thams cad rnam 'phrul bas//sems can don kun rnam 'phrul na//myur du shin tu 'grub par 
11a2(rgyud): 'gyur//nyin re 'am yang na zla re 'am//yang na de bzhin lo rer ni//ji bzhin byin gyis brlabs nas kyang //sangs rgyas bde mchog gang bya'o//khams kyi ngo bo thams cad 
11a3(rgyud): dang //de bzhin srog dang rtsol las byung //rang gi lha yi cha lugs su//grub pa'i phyag rgyar rab tu bya//dkyil 'khor gang dang gang brten pa//sna tshogs phyag rgya'i mchod 
11a4(rgyud): pa yis //slob ma'i tshogs kun de dang der//mchod pa byas nas der sgrub po//de las gzhan yang rtog kun gyi//sangs rgyas thams cad ram 'phrul ba//ci rigs par ni sprul 
11a5(rgyud): byas pa'i//btam dag rab tu bshad par bya//sems dpa' sangs rgyas kun gyi dngos//rdo rje sems dpa' bde ba'i mchog//rtag tu da ltar byung ba bzhin//ngo bo nyid ni 'da' bar 
11a6(rgyud): dka'//cho ga sna tshogs 'dod rnams la//las kyi sbyor ba sna tshogs kyi//sangs rgyas rdo rje 'dzin lasogs//'dul ba'i phyir ni byas pa yin//sangs rgyas lasogs brtan g.yo 
11a7(rgyud): kun//kun gyi dngos grub 'di 'gyur ro//sangs rgyas thams cad mnyam sbyor ba//mkha' 'gro sgyu ma bde ba'i mchog//sgyu ma'i sbyor ba 'di yis ni//rnam pa sna tshogs kun gyi 
11b1(rgyud): mchog //sems can kun gyi don mchog rnams//sangs rgyas la sogs 'dul bas 'grub//dpal rdo rje sems dpas dbugs phyung ba'o////'das pa'i dus na sngon byung ba//

19a1(rgyud): mnyaM sbyor @#/ O /glu 'di yis ni bskul bar bya//rjes su chags pa rnams mang dga' ba'i pad ma ni//rnam dag mchod pas gsal bar rjes su mchod byas ba//
19a2(rgyud): mchog tu rtag pa'i rnal 'byor bde bas bde 'grub mdzod//kye ma mchog tu bde ba'i dam tshig ngo bo nyid//mchog tu bde ba shin tu rnal 'byor bskal bzang mchog dgyes pa//rang gi dam 
19a3(rgyud): tshig skyon med rtag la rol cig rol cig ho//kye ma mchog tu gsang ba'i bdag po rang gi dam tshig ni//yin pa'i phyir na rab tu gsang ba'i lha mchog rnams so//'gro ba'i rgyud ni rnams 
19a4(rgyud): pa sna tshogs 'dul ba gsal bar mkhyen//dam tshig dngos po brtan g.yo kun gyi ngo bor gyur rgyal ba//kun gyi bdag nyid rnal 'byor bde ba mkha' 'gro sgyu ma'o//kye ma dam pa 
19a5(rgyud): brtag pa'i dngos grub chen po rab bsgrubs ston//dzaHhUM baM hoHe ma ho//bde mchog bde bas drang du gsol//rnam par sprul cing rnam par mdzad//a la la ho//de bzhin

19b1(rgyud): pa med par snang //rnam pa mang po'i 'dod chags rgyud kyi sems//ma lus lus pa med par 'dul mkhas pa//sgyu ma bde mchog de bzhin gshegs kun gyi//sgyu mas thams cad rab 
19b2(rgyud): sprul rnam par 'phrul//te na te te hUM//rnam pa mang po'i dga' ba'i rgyud kyi sems//ma lus las pa med par 'dul mkhas//sgyu ma bde mchog de bzhIn gshegs kun gyi//sgyu 
19b3(rgyud): ma thams cad rab sprul rnam par sprul//hUM hUM hUM//rnam pa mang pa'i khro bo'i rgyud kyi sems//ma luslus pa med par 'dul mkha' pa//sgyu ma bde mchog debazhin gshegs kun 
19b4(rgyud): gyi//sgyu mas thams cad rang sprul rnam par sprul//ru lu ru lu hUM b+h+yo hUM//rnam pa mang po'i srog gi rgyud kyi sems//ma lus lus pa med par 'dul mkhas pa//sgyu ma bde mtshog 
19b5(rgyud): de bzhin gshegs kun gyi//sgyu mas thams cad rab sprul rnamapar sprul//hUM shiSh+yA bi sha hUM//de lasogs pa mtha' yas mchog/chos dbyings mnyam dang mi mnyam pa//namakha'i

19b7(rgyud): bdag nyid 'grub par 'gyur//glu 'di dag ni blangs pa dang //lha mo kun gyis rab bskul nas//mkha' 'gro sgyu ma bde ba'i mchog/sangs rgyas kun gyi bdag nyid 'grub//dbang po
20a1(rgyud): mnyaM sbyor @#/ O /kun las byung ba yi//mchod pa'i sprin chen rnam bkod pa//rab 'byam tshogs rnams thams cad kyis//kun gyi bdag nyid mchod par byed //sangs rgyas 
20a2(rgyud): thams cad mnyam sbyor ba//mkha' 'gro sgyu ma bde mchog gis//dngos grub kun gyi bdag nyid ni//ma lus lus pa med par 'grub//ston pa'i phyag rgya phab pa yi//bud med dag 
20a3(rgyud): kyang bsgrubs nas ni//rdo rjer bcas pa'i mtshan mar 'dzin//rnal 'byor mar ni rab tu 'gyur//de dag rang gi lha rnams dang //'dra bar byin gyis brlabs bsgrubs nas//sngon 
20a4(rgyud): med rol mo sil snyan gar//rnam par sprul pas 'grub par 'gyur//lha'i gzugs dang sgra dang dri//ro dang reg pa bde mos pa//bdag nyid rang gi lhar mthong zhing //mchod pa 
20a5(rgyud): dag kyang byed par 'gyur//lha yi mchod pa mnyam sbyor ba//bskal bzang mchog gi 'dod chags can//rnam pa kun tu bskal bzang po//bde ba yang dag bsgrub par byed//
20a6(rgyud): sangs rgyas sgyu mas byin rlabs pas//rdo rje sems dpa' mnyam sbyor ba'i//dngos grub kun gyi bde ba rnams//dkyil 'khor mthong bas myong bar 'gyur//rdo rje sems 
20a7(rgyud): dpar sbyor ba yis//mthu nyid kyis ni thams cad du//sna tshogs kun gyi bskal bzang po//rang gi bde ba nyid grub po//sangs rgyas kun gyi gzungs chen dang //
20b1(rgyud): dbyangs dang dri dang ro bde bas//rdo rje sems dpa' bdag nyid kyis//de yis rab mthong mchod pa byed//mchod pa thams cad mnyam sbyor ba//bskal bzang mchog gi 'dod 
20b2(rgyud): chags can//sangs rgyas thams cad bdag nyid kyi//dngos grub ma lus lus med 'grub//sangs rgyas thams cad mnyam sbyor ba//mkha' 'gro sgyu ma bde mchog gi//sangs 
20b3(rgyud): rgyas kun gyi bdag nyid gar//de nas yang dag rang gsang bas//bud med dag ni bzhi rnams dang //gar gyi phyag rgya mchod pa yis//mchod pa thams cad dbul bar bya//
20b4(rgyud): rab tu dga' ba'i bdag la phyag 'tshal lo//de bzhin gshegs pa dam pa grub par mdzod//oM AHhUM/de bzhin gshegs pa dmapa grub par mdzod//oM AHhUM/
20b5(rgyud): dam tshig dam pa grub par mdzad du gsol//mchod pa chen po thams cad dga' bar mdzod//oM AHhUM//mchod pa chen po thams chad grub par mdzod//
20b6(rgyud): oM AHhUM//glu yi phyag rgya 'di ni dpal//rdo rje sems dpa' la gnas te//mchod pa ma lus lus med pa'i//rab 'byam las la sbyar du rung //gang dag grub 
20b7(rgyud): par ma gyur dang //mchod pa dag ni mi 'byor pa//phyag rgya 'di yis mchod byas na//mchod pa thams cad myur du 'grub//mchod pa'i sbyor ba 'di dag dang //
21a1(rgyud): mnyam sbyor @#/ O /lha mo bzhi po dag gis ni//me tog la sogs mchod byas nas//slob ma thams cad bzung bar bya//dngos grub 'dod pas 
21a2(rgyud): ci 'dod par //snyam pa'i gar ni kun bskyod nas//de dag gdong ni mdzes pa yi//ras kyis g.yogs nas bzung par bya//dngos grub ji ltar thob 'dod par//
21a3(rgyud): slob ma me tog dor du bzhug/lha mo gang la ltung ba ni//lha de de la 'grub par 'gyur//de nas gdong ni bkrol nas kyang //yon gyi mchog ni blang bar bya//sangs rgyas 
21a4(rgyud): rin chen dbang bskur bas//ji lta bar ni dbang bskur ro//dmar chen po dang ga bur bcas//tsan dan dmar po sbyar ba rnams//tshogs kyi dbus su bzhag pa la//
21a5(rgyud): g.yon pa mtshan 'dzin rdo rjer bcas//rang gi mtshan ni rnal 'byor ldan//srin lag dag ni mthe bo'i rtses//zhi ba'i btung ba bzhin myangs na//rtag pa'i dngos grub thob 
21a6(rgyud): par 'gyur//sangs rgyas thams cad mnyam sbyor ba//mkha' 'gro sgyu ma tshogs kyis kyang //rgyas mi thebs shing mi rmongs te//de la gnod par mi 'gyur ro//de nas 
21a7(rgyud): gna' ni bgegs byas nas//sangs rgyas thams cad mthun pa yis//mchog tu rtag pa'i bka' stsal pa//dam tshig sdom pa sbyin par bya//sangs rgyas thams cad
21b1(rgyud): mnyam sbyar ba'i//sdom pa nyams par khyed byed pa//ji snyed sangs rgyas thams cad dang //byang chub sems dpa' rdo rje 'dzin//lha dag dang ni mi rnams dang //de bzhin 
21b2(rgyud): rnal 'byor grub pa po//de dag la ni snying rings nas//snying la khrag chen 'thung ba zhig/sems can khams ni ma lus don//srid pa shin tu dag bya'i phyir//
21b3(rgyud): mi 'gog chos nyid sbyor ba yis//rtag pa nyid du bsgrub par bya//bdag gis ngo bo yangs spangs shing //dka' thub gyis ni gzir mi bya//ci bde dag 
21b4(rgyud): ni bde bar spyad//'di ni phyi dus sangs rgyas yin//rnal 'byor longs spyod thams cad la//cis kyang ma 'jigs dga' bar gyis//sdig pa med 
21b5(rgyud): kyis khyed ma 'jigs//dam tshig 'da' bar dka' ba'o//grub pa gcig pu ci la spyod//lhan cig spyod dang rjes spyod 'jug/sangs rgyas

22a1(rgyud): @#//yang dag rdzogs pa'i sangs rgyas dang //rdo rje can gyis bka' stsal to//rnal 'byor bcas dang gsang bcas pa'i//

22a3(rgyud): phyag rgya'i ye shes rjes bzung bas//rnam par snang mdzad mchog bzhin du//sangs rgyas kun gyi bdag nyid 'grub//de nas ro dang zas dang gnas//
22a4(rgyud): glu dang sil snyan la sogs pa//de dag mgon po rnams la dbul//rdo rje bla ma chen po dang //bdag dang gzhan la 'dod dgus mchod//de nas bdag 
22a5(rgyud): nyid rab bsgrubs nas//kun gyi slob dpon rab gnas pa//ji lta'i tshul gyis 'jug pa yi//bden pa'i chos ni brjod par bya//rdo rje sems 
22a6(rgyud): dpa' dpal gyi tshul//rtog pa thams cad rab 'grub cing //rtog pa kun la rab sbyor ba'i//ngo bo nyid phyir chen po'i mchog/sna tshogs las kyi sbyor ba dang //
22a7(rgyud): 'dul ba'i bdag nyid sna tshogs pas//sems can rnams ni 'dul ba'i phyir//de las gzhan pa'i tshul du bstan//rnal 'byor de nyid bcom ldan 'das//rdo rje sems dpa' de bzhin 
22b1(rgyud): gshegs//thar pa thams cad rnam sbyang phyir//grol ba'i mkha' 'gro 'byung bar 'gyur//rang gi lha dang 'dra ba yin//rang gis byin brlabs bsgrub pa'i rgya//phyag rgya ji bzhin rjes 'thun 
22b2(rgyud): pa'i//sangs rgyas sprul pa bsgrub par bya//gzhan yang rnal 'byor bcom ldan 'das//dam tshig la sdang rnam sbyang phyir//thams cad rnal 'byor dbang phyug mchog/rdo rje mkha' 'gro 'byung bar 'gyuro/
22b3(rgyud): gzhan yang shin tu rnal 'byor pa//ma lus pad mo rnam sbyang phyir//bskal bzang dam pa mi 'gyur ba//pad ma sgyu ma 'byung bar 'gyur//gzhan yang rnal 'byor pa bcom ldan 'das//nor gyi mchog ni rnam sbyang 
22b4(rgyud): phyir//namakha' kun gyi bdag po che//rdo rje nyi ma dpal 'byung 'gyur//rnal 'byor de nyid bcom ldan 'das//drag shul sbyor ba sbyang ba'i phyir//rta 'grin pha rol rab gnon pa//rta mchog rab tu 'byung bar 'gyur//
22b5(rgyud): rang gi lha dang 'dra ba yi//rang gi byin brlabs bsgrub pa'i phyir//gzugs brnyan ngo bor sbyor ba'o//phyag rgya'i sbyor bas gar bya'o//dam tshig rnam pa sna tshogs kyis//de ltar rnam par spyad na yang //sangs rgyas 
22b6(rgyud): thams cad mnyam sbyor ba//mkha' 'gro sgyu ma bde mchog 'grub//kun du kun nas thams cad ni//sangs rgyas 'phrul pa thams cad kyis//bdag nyid rtag tu 
22b7(rgyud): rnam pa kun//kun gyi dngos por de 'gyur ro//kun tu sangs rgyas thams cad ni//sangs rgas rnam 'phrul thams cad kyis//bdag gnyid rnam pa 'phrul pa ston//
22b8(rgyud): lha mo kun dang 'dus pa yis//sangs rgyas sprul pa grub par byed//sangs rgyas sprul pa sgyu ma yis//sangs rgyas rnams kyi mdzad pa ston//sangs rgyas thams cad mnyam sbyor ba'i//
23a1(rgyud): mnyam sbyor @#/ O /mkha' 'gro sgyu ma gar byed pas//khams gsum dag ni ma lus par //dngos bor kun tu de 'gyur ro//pad ma sgyu ma rnam 'phrul bas //sangs rgyas rdo rje 'dzin la sogs //
23a2(rgyud): sna tshogs gzugs byed rim pa yis //'gro ba thams cad mgu bar byed //khams gsum pa yi rgyal srid che//'jal sdums g.yul 'gyed grub pa dag /rgyal srid sgyu ma rnam 'phrul bas//bya ba 
23a3(rgyud): thams cad ston par byed//khams gsum pa'i rgyal srid che//bdag rtul phod pas mnan nas ni//'gro ba thams cad rab 'joms pas//rta'i gar gyis ston par byed//de la sogs ba mtha' 
23a4(rgyud): yas mchog /chos dbyings mnyam dang mi mnyam pa//namakha'i dbyings kyi mtha' klas pa'i //sna tshogs kun gyi bde mchog gis//namakha' kun gyi skabs na dpal//rdo rje sems

23b1(rgyud): dpas gsungs so////sangs rgyas thams cad dang mnyam par sbyor ba //mkha'agro ma sgyu ma bde mchog las/sangs rgyas thams cad dang gnyam par sbyor ba //mkha' 'gro ma

23b5(rgyud): rtse rnams thams cad du //rnam pa du ma sna tshogs kyis//sangs rgyas dgongs pa bsgrub par bya//dur khrod dang ni lha rten dang //ma mo'i gnas sogs 'dug na dpal //rdo rje

23b7(rgyud): par ni 'grub par 'gyur//rgyal po'i pho brang dga' ba dang //rgyal po'i skyed mos tshal dag tu//rnam pa sna tshogs du ma yi//rgyal po'i dgos pa bsgrub par bya//'khor ni rnam
24a1(rgyud): mnyam sbyor @#/ O /pa sna tshogs dang //de bzhin chu bo'i 'gram dag gam//rgyal khams gzhan las grub pa ru//rdo rje rta ni bsgrub par bya//cha lugs rnam pa sna tshogs dang //
24a2(rgyud): bsgrub pa sna tshogs rnams kyi dpal //rdo rje sems dpa'i dgos pa la//dkyil 'khor la sogs rab tu bsgrubs//sangs rgyas rnams kyi pad ma dmar//phyag na rdo rje sna tshogs pa//
24a3(rgyud): 'jig rten dbang gi kha dog dkar//rdo rje nyi ma dpal gyi gser//kha dog kun dang ci 'byor pa//bla res rgyal mtshan rgyan rnam pa//thams cad rab tu bsgrub pa dpal//rta mchog rdo rje can gyi'o
24a4(rgyud): gnas dang 'khor rnams ci 'dra dang//bkod dang 'gro ba ci 'dra dang//'khor rnams dkod pa ci 'dra bar//de 'dri'i 'khor lo mchog gis mdzad //rdo rje sems dpa'i lha rnams kyi//

24a7(rgyud): gyis//dkyil 'khor brtag pa 'grub par 'gyur//bdag nyid rang gi lhar sbyor bas//dngos grub thams cad bsgrub par bya//cho ga lhag gam ma tshang na 'ang //nyes pa med par 'grub 
24b1(rgyud): bar 'gyur//kun tu sna tshogs bskal bzang po//kun tu sna tshogs 'khor lo can//kun tu sna tshogs 'grub pa'i phyir//dkyil 'khor kun tu sna tshogs pa'o//dkyil 'khor 
24b2(rgyud): zlum po sangs rgyas kyi//rdo rje can gyi zla gam bzhin//pad ma dbang gi pad ma'i dbyibs//rin chen mchog gi rin chen dbyibs//ji ltar 'dod pa ci 'byor pa//ji lta'i dus dang ci 
24b3(rgyud): bde bar//dkyil 'khor kun tu gnas pa dpal//rta mchog phyag rgya rdo rje yi//dkyil 'khor du ni ma zhugs shing //dam tshig rnams ni spangs pa dang //gsang ba yang 
24b4(rgyud): dag mi shes pas//bsgrub pa ci yang mi 'grub po//srog chags bsad par mi bya zhing //dkon mchog gsum ni yong mi spang //byang chub sems kyang mi btang ste//slob 
24b5(rgyud): dpon skal bzang yang mi btang //kun tu sna tshogs bskalabazang ba//bdag med chos nyid dang ldan pa//dam tshig la sdang bskal ba ngan//srid pa shin tu bsgrub pas

24b7(rgyud): bskal bzang phyag na rdo rje dpal//stong pa nyid la rab sbyor bas//rang rgyal rnams ni bskal ba ngan//'dod chags thams cad rnam dag pas//bskal bzang 
25a1(rgyud): mnyam sbyor @#/ O /spyan ras gzigs kyi dbang //log par lta dang ldan pas na//bskal ngan mu stegs 'jig rten pa//dam pa'i nor mchog bsdud pa'i 
25a2(rgyud): phyir//bskal bzang rdo rje nyi ma dpal//bdag gi bu la 'ang ma chags pas//bskal ngan dka' thub spyod pa kun //rgyal srid mchog rnams kun bsdus pas///rta 
25a3(rgyud): mchog dpal ni bskal ba bzang //bdag med yongs su 'dzin med pas//thub pa thams cad bskal ba ngan//bde ba thams cad bdag bsgrub phyir//kun gyi slob 
25a4(rgyud): dpon bskal ba bzang //ltas ngan dam tshig la sdang ba'i//slob ma thams cad bskal ba ngan//gsang ba'i don de yang dag nyid//'dir ni gsal bar shes pa yis//
25a5(rgyud): sangs rgyas kun gyi bka' bzhin du//sangs rgyas kun tu 'grub par 'gyur//kun gyi slob dpon la gnas pas//'khor lo 'jug par byed pa na//dkyil 'khor 'dod 
25a6(rgyud): pa'i slob ma la //gsang ba yang dag mtha' yas mchog/thog ma nyid du gsal brjod pa//kye khyod ci la dga' zhes dri//bskal pa bzang la bdag dga' zhes//'jug pa pos 
25a7(rgyud): kyang smra bar bya//gsang ba'i yan lag 'di shes na//ji ltar 'dod pa rab tu bzhug/gsang ba yang dag mi shes na///g.yas pa yis ni bzhug par bya//de ltar dkyil 'khor du 
25b1(rgyud): zhugs nas//de bzhin du ni yon phul te//gsang ba'i cho ga nyid ces pas //mi 'gyur ba'i mchog 'grub po//kun tu sna tshogs bla ma la//yon mchog dag ni dbul bar nges//
25b2(rgyud): gang la yongs su btang dka' gang//de ni mchog tu bshad pa yin//gang gi sdug pa'i mchog/yang na shin tu khyad par can//de dang de ni bla ma la//de nyid mi zad 'dod
25b3(rgyud): pas dbul//yang na de nyid 'dod 'gyur ba 'am//'on te sems la dgos pa dag/de sbyin yang na dgos par bya//de ni chung yang mchog yin no//de ni gal te mchog bdog
25b4(rgyud): la//de yang ngan pa'i yon phul na//kun gyi slob dpon brnyas pas na//de nyid rtag tu sdug bsngal thob //bu mo shin tu brgyan pa 'am//de bzhin chung ma sdug pa dag//phul
25b5(rgyud): nas rin gyis blu ba ni//sangs rgyas byang chub mchog gi yon //mi chen bdag gis phul nas su//slar yang rin gyis blu ba ni//rdo rje 'dzin dpal sbyor ba yin //yon de mchog
25b6(rgyud): gi yon yin no//mu tig bzang dang nor bu dang//shin tu dkar ba'i ras zung dang//de bzhin rta ni dkar po dag/pad mo can gyi yon mchog yin//rgyal srid longs spyod gter rnams dang //
25b7(rgyud): gos mchog rgyan gyi tshogs rnams dang //de bzhin mchod pa thams cad ni//rdo rje nyi ma'i yon yin no//shin tu brtan pa'i rta dag dang //glang po shin tu brgyan pa dang//shing rta rin chen
26a1(rgyud): mnya sbyor @#/ O /gang ba dang //rta'i rgyal po'i yon yin no//khams kyi rang bzhin thams cad dang //srog dang rtsa'i de bzhin te//sangs rgyas rin chen dbang bskur ba //
26a2(rgyud): mkhas pa rnams pa sna tshogs brtag /sbyi bo dang ni dpral ba dang//ldan pa gnyis dang lhag pa ru//sangs rgyas rin chen thams cad kyi//dbu rgyan mi 'gyur dam pa'o//khams kyi 
26a3(rgyud): rang bzhin thams cad dam//srog dang rtsa yi de bzhin te//thod pa rin chen dbu rgyan nam//sangs rgyas gtsug gi nor bu dag/kun tu sna tshogs bla mar ni//kun gyi slob dpon 
26a4(rgyud): kun la bya//rgyal srid thams cad rnam dag la//ma lus lus pa med par 'grub//rin chen ngo mtshar gcig las byas//de bzhin sna tshogs rgyan gyis spras//sangs rgyas bad mos
26a5(rgyud): dbang bskur bas//slob ma thams cad dbang bskur ro//sangs rgyas rin chen rdo rje phyed //rdo rje'i rigs la rdo rje yin//spyan ras gzigs kyi rdo rje sku//nor bu 'dzin gyi nor bu yin//
26a6(rgyud): sangs rgyas rin chen pad mo dang //rdo rje nor bu de bzhin du//thams cad mchog ni rta mchog dpal//rang gi dbang bskur bsdus pa'o//blugs ma 'am ni byas pa 'am //brdungs pa 
26a7(rgyud): 'am ni bris kyang rung//sna tshogs rab tu legs pa yi//mtshan ma'i phyag rgya rab tu brtag/kun tu sna tshogs bskal bzang po//rang byung rnams kyi mtshan ma'i rgya//nam mkha'i 
26b1(rgyud): dbyings kyi mtha' klas pa'i//lha mo rnams kyi bde ba mchog/gzhu dang mda' dang de bzhin du //bskal bzang de ni bskal ba bzang //thod pa dang ni kha T+wAM khwa//rdo rje rgya gram de 
26b2(rgyud): bzhin no//pad mo rgya gram dpag med tshe //sangs rgyas kun gyi tshe bsgrubs pa //lha mo'i tshogs rnams thams cad kyi//mtshan ma'i phyag rgya tshogs bsgrub bzhin //mda' dang 
26b3(rgyud): de bzhin tshangs pa'i mgo//de bzhin 'khor lo'i phyag rgya dang //rdo rje tri shul phyag rgya'i mtshan //dpag bsam shing dang lcug ma dang //pad mo rta 'grin 'phang mtho ba//rta  
26b4(rgyud): mgrin ljang khu de bzhin no//phag gi mche ba grub pa dang //zla ba nyi ma de bzhin no//rin chen sna tshogs snod chen du//sangs rgyas gzugs kyi ra sa yan//thams cad 
26b5(rgyud): bdag nyid myong bskal bzang //mtshan ma'i phyag rgya sna tshogs bzhag/gling bu dang ni pi wang kun //mu kun ta dang mu ra dza //me tog bdug pa mar me dang //sna tshogs
26b6(rgyud): dri rnams de bzhin no//de la sogs te mtha' yas mchog/chos dbyings mnyam dang mi mnyam pa//nam mkha'i dbyings kyi mtha' klas pa'i //mtshan ma'i phyag rgya dam pa'i 
26b7(rgyud): tshogs //nam mkha' kun gyi skabs na dpal//rdo rje sems dpa' nyid la gnas//grub pa'i dbang phyug rab grub pas //thams cad bdag nyid grub par 'gyur//chos 
27a1(rgyud): mnyam sbyor @#/ O /gos dang ni lhung bzed dang //de bzhin du ni khar sil dang //dam pa'i chos kyi glegs bam dang //mtshan ma'i phyag rgya'i tshogs bzhan no//de bzhin lce'i phyag rgya dang //
27a2(rgyud): cha lang dang ni rnga dag dang //rdo rje dang ni rin chen snod//mtshan ma'i phyag rgya sogs gzhan no//de bzhin sangs rgyas gzugs brnyan dang //me long dang ni rnga yab dang //rdo rje ser dang 'phrin 
27a3(rgyud): las bcas//de bzhin mig gi phyag rgya dang //rdo rje 'bar dang kha T+wAM kA/thod pa kham phor de bzhin no//dmar chen dang ni snying chen dang //sha chen po 'ang de bzhin te//rnon po mda' dang 
27a4(rgyud): de bzhin 'dren //chang dang chu yang de bzhin te//lcug ma rlung gis bskyod pa dang //ras dang de bzhin me dag ni//rdo rje khro bo'i rgyal po'i dpal//bdug pa thams cad za ba yi//mtshan mI
27a5(rgyud): phyag rgya grub pa'i tshogs //rnal 'byor dngos grub rab bsgrub pa'o//pad ma sna tshogs dmar po dang //ljang khu de bzhin dkar po dang //'thing ka ser po leb rgan dag/pad 
27a6(rgyud): ma'i mtshan mchog rnam pa bdun//pad ma sbyang po bud med brgya//de bzhin chos kyi 'khor lo dang //gsal zhing dpal bskyed rdo rje dag/pad mo mtshan ma'i tshogs gzhan no//gzhu 
27a7(rgyud): dang mda' dang ral gri dang //de bzhin du ni 'bar ba'i me //dbang bskur gter dang rgyal mtshan dag//mchod pa'i mtshan mas bsdus pa'o//de bzhin sna tshogs pad mo dang //
27b1(rgyud): rdo rje rgya gram de bzhin te/mtshan ma'i phyag rgya 'grub pa gnyis//rgyal srid thams cad 'joms pa ste/sangs rgyas thams cad lasogs pas/dngos po bstan g.yo ma lus kun//rang 
27b2(rgyud): byung rnams kyi mtshan ma'i rgya/namakha'i dbyings kyi mtha' klas pa'o/de skad bcom ldan de bzhin gshegs/rdo rje sems dpa' dpal gyis gsungs/sangs rgyas thams cad dang mnyaM
27b3(rgyud): par sbyor ba//mkha' 'gro ma sgyu ma bde ba'i mchog las/dkyil 'khor gyi phyag rgya shes pa'i brtag pa ste////bdun pa'o////de nas gzhan
27b4(rgyud): yang bshad pa ni/rdo rje sems dpa' dpalagyi lha/rnam pa kun tu sna tshogs mchog/mchod pa thams cad rnam sprul pa'o/lag pa g.yas g.yon gnyis dag gis//'jub
27b5(rgyud): mo mthe'u chung mdzes par bsgyur/dpal ldan g.yon pa 'gying pa'i thabs/g.yas pa ba dan gsum par bya/srid zhur bcas pa steg pa'i mchog/bdag nyid rdo rje sems
27b6(rgyud): dpa' dpal//'dzum dang bcas pa'i mig gis blta//bsam pa g.yos pa'i phyag bya'o/sangs rgyas rdo rje pad mo 'dzin/rdo rje nyi ma khams gsum pas/skad cig gis 
27b7(rgyud): kyang phyag byed na/khengs pa gzhan lta ci zhig smos/drag po dbang po nye dbang bcas/nyi zla gshin rje tshangs par bcas/btud de rab tu phyag 'tshal nas/las 
28a1(rgyud): mnyam sbyor @#/ O /rnam(sa) thams cad byed par 'gyur//rdo rje dpal gyis thal mo bsgrub/bsam pa g.yon pas phyag byas pa'i//sangs rgyas mchod pa legs byas na//ma lus lus 
28a2(rgyud): pa med par 'grub//smin ma g.yon pa 'degs tshul gyis//ru lu ru lur bcas dgod na//tshangs pa nye dbang drag por bcas//khams gsum pa rnams phyag byed 'gyur//thal mo pad ma 
28a3(rgyud): kha 'bus bzhin//rang nyid sbyor ldan snying khar bcing //mthe bo mthe chung 'dab ma bzhin//rgyas btud pa yi phyag kyang bya//lus kyi yan lag thams cad ni//sa la bsnyal te phyag bya ba//
28a4(rgyud): snying kha dang/ni spyi gtsug tu//thal mo sbyar te phyag bya'o//dpal ldan rdo rje 'gying ba'i thabs//'dzum pa'i mig gis phyag byas nas//khams gsum pa yang phyag byed na//mthus snyems 
28a5(rgyud): gzhan lta ci zhig dgos//phyag 'tshal phyag rgya shes pa'o//gar gyi rgyud rnams ji snyed pa//de bzhin bya ba'i tshul ston kun//de dag rdo rje sems dpa'i rgya//rang gi don rnams 'grub byed 
28a6(rgyud): pa//'gying dang bcas pa'i tshul byas nas//ji ltar tshul bzhin gar byed cing //bskor ba'i gar rnams sna tshogs kyis///mchod pa sna tshogs dbul bar bya//lag pa g.yas g.yon gnyis 
28a7(rgyud): dag gi/'dzub mo the chung mdzes par bsgyur//g.yon pa'i 'dzub mo khu tshur bcangs//g.yas pa ba dan gsum par bya//phyag rgya 'di yis btab na ni//sangs rgyas kun dang 'grogs 
28b1(rgyud): pa dang//dngos grub chen po mchog kun gyi//dbang phyug chen po 'grub par 'gyur//sangs rgyas thams cad dang 'grogs pa//dpal bskal pa bzang po zhes bya ba'i phyag rgya'o//snyems 'dzin
28b2(rgyud): chen por bcas pa yi//sbyor bas de ni btegs nas su//'gram pa'i phyogs su yongs bsgyur nas//spyi bo'i 'tshams su bzhag par bya//thams cad 'bebs pa'i gar byed pas//gar byas
28b3(rgyud): de nas phab nas su//bskal bzang hUM zhes bya ba'i glus//mchod pa thams cad dbul bar bya//sangs rgyas thams cad mnyam par sbyor ba//dpal bskal pa bzang bo zhes bya ba'i   
28b4(rgyud): phyag rgya'o//lag pa g.yas g.yon gnyis dag gis//'dzub mo mthe gnyis mdzes bsgyur ba'i//rdo rje thal mo sbyar nas su //de bzhin g.yon du bsgyur bar bya'o//de ni sbyor bas bteg nas ni//
28b5(rgyud): bskal bzang o zhes bya ba'i glus//mchong pa thams cad dbul bar bya//dpal rdo rje thal mo bsgyur bsangs rgyas kyi mchod pa'o//lag pa g.yas g.yon gnyis dag gis//'dzub mo mthe 
28b6(rgyud): chung mdzes bsgyur te//rdo rje thal mo gyen du dgrol //mchod pa'i gtor ma kun gyi tshul//dur khrod cha lug gar byed pas/phyag rgya 'di yis gar byed cing //bskal bzang rdo rje drag por 'god //
28b7(rgyud): mchod pa thams cad dbul bar bya//dpal rdo rje he ru ka'i thal mo ste mchod pa'i gtor ma thams cad kyi cho ga'o //lag pa g.yas g.yon gnyas dag gis //'dzub mo mthe chung mdzes 
29a1(rgyud): bsgyur te/g.yas g.yon thal mo brdabs pa yis/mchod pa thams cad dbul bar bya/dpal pad ma gar gyi dbang phyug gis thal mo'o/lag pa g.yas g.yon gnyis 
29a2(rgyud): dag gis/'dzub mo mthe chung mdzes bsgyur te/rdo rje rin chen thal sbyar nas/mchod pa thams cad 'bul bar bya/re ba thams cad yongs su rdzogs par byed pa dpal rdo rje rin chen zhes 
29a3(rgyud): bya ba'i phyag rgya'o/gyen du mthor zhing thur du 'bebs/bskor zhing de bzhin sbub pa dang /me tog bdug pa mar me mchog/dri yi mchod pa 'byung ba'o//gang ba 'am 'on te stong 
29a4(rgyud): pa 'ang rung /yang na ci 'byor pa yis yang /dam tshig nyams par bsgrubs pa yis/mchod pa tham cad 'grub par 'gyur/phyag rgya 'di rnams bcings nas/ni/sangs rgyas thams cad 
29a5(rgyud): mchod par 'gyur/dngos grub dbang phyug grub pas kyang /kun gyi bdag nyid mchod par 'gyur/chos dbyings mnyam dang mi mnyam pa/nam mkha'i dbyings kyi mtha' klas pa'i/rang 
29a6(rgyud): byung kun gyi mchod pa kun/gang dag de yis ma byas shing /spel bar yang ni ma gyur pa'i/rlabs chen dam pa gang yang med/sangs rgyas thams cad mnyam sbyor ba/'byor pa'i 
29a7(rgyud): dbang phyug bde chen dang /dngos grub las dang bde ba dag/'di yis mi 'grub gang yang med/mchod pa rab 'byam grub pa rnams/ma lus lus pa med par 'grub/ces dpal 
29b1(rgyud): bcom ldan 'das rdo rje sems dpas gsungs so////sangs rgyas thams cad dang mnyam par sbyor ba/mkha'agro ma sgyu ma bde ba'i mchog las//mchod pa thams cad dang mnyam
29b2(rgyud): par sbyor ba//dpal bskal pa bzang po'i dam tshig gi rtog pa ste///brgyad pa'o////de nas gzhan yang bshad pa ni//rdo rje sems dpa' dpal
29b3(rgyud): gyi lha//kun tu sna tshogs mchog rnams kyi//phyag rgya thams cad rnam sprul pa'o//sems dpa' sangs rgyas kun gyi dngos//rdo rje sems dpa' bde ba'i mchog/gsang ba'i mchog
29b4(rgyud): ni dges pa na/thams cad bdag nyid rtag tu bzhugs//bde ba kun la zhen pa dang //dngos grub thams cad rnam dag phyir//rdo rje sems dpa' bdeba'i mchog /zla ba nya ltar gtsang
29b5(rgyud): ma'i sku//'dod chags kun la chags pa'i phyir //chags pa chen po bcom ldan 'das /rdo rje sems dpa'i phyag rgya 'di//dmar po sna tshogs 'byung ba'i 'od//khams gsum dag
29b6(rgyud): pa rin chen gzhan//sangs rgyas rin chen lta bu med//sangs rgyas kun gyi rin chen che//dbu rgyan can ni de bas mchog/gzhan gyi nor min mi phrogs pa//lha'i chu yis dag pa ste//
29b7(rgyud): mthu dang ngo bo nyid dag dang //rang bzhin gyis ni de grub pa'o//thod pa'i phreng ba'i dbu rgyan dang //sangs rgyas gtsug gi nor bu can //kun nas nyon mongs 'jig rten dbang //
30a1(rgyud): mnyam sbyor @#/ O /mi 'jed la sogs de grub pa'o//rdo rje sems dpa' dpal sbyor ba//lha mo kun dang 'dus pa yis//kun tu sna tshogs bskal bzang ba//mkha'agro ma ni thams
30a2(rgyud): cad 'grub //sangs rgyas rdo rje pad mo 'dzin //rdo rje nyi ma khams gsum pa//rgyas btab rmongs byas bslang nas ni//las rnams kun la sbyor bar byed//phyag rgya 'di yis btab nas ni//
30a3(rgyud): 'dod pa bzhin du 'gro 'ong lding //lha mo kun mchog dpal dang ldan//rdo rje sems dpa' rab tu 'grub //mchog tu dga' ba la rab tu sbyor ba bskal pa bzang po dpal rdo rje sems dpa' 
30a4(rgyud): bde ba'i mchog rol pa zhes bya ba'i phyag rgya'o//g.yas dang g.yon pa'i sbyor ba yi//bde ba'i cha lugs gar byed cing //sems dpa'i dkyil mo dkrung bcas//nas//dpung rnams 
30a5(rgyud): thams cad rab tu 'joms//dpal rdo rje sems dpa'i dkyil mo dkrud ngo//zla ba'i kha dog 'dzum pa'i zhal//dngos grub thams cad dag pa dang//pad ma'i 'dod chags rnam dag 
30a6(rgyud): phyir//brla'i dbus ni dmar la dkar //phyag mchog lo ma sngon po 'dra//de bzhin bde ba'i dbang phyug dang//bde bar reg pa'i dbang phyug pas//dkar zhing ser ba'i brla dang 
30a7(rgyud): ldan//lha rdzas sna tshogs kha dog mchog//'bar ba sna tshogs 'khrug pa'i 'od//pad ma sna tshogs la gnas pa//thams cad dag par rab tu gnas //sangs rgyas pad mo la sog+sa
30b1(rgyud): mchog/rin chen sna tshogs gis brgyan dang/sangs rgyas thams cad 'dus pa yis//mi 'jed la sogs brgya byin bzhin //bya ba dang ni bzlog pa dang//'phrog pa dag kyang lhag
30b2(rgyud): par mdzad //bdag la 'khrud pa'i phyag rgya yis//yan lag thams cad phrogs par 'grub //phyag rgya 'di yis btab na ni //'dod pa bzhin du 'gro 'ong lding //dpal ldan 'dod
30b3(rgyud): pa kun la dbang //rdo rje sems dpa' rab tu 'grub//mchog tu dga' la rab tu sbyor ba bskal ba bzang po dpal rdo rje bde ba'i mchog rol pa zhes bya ba'i phyag rgya'o//rnam pa sna tshogs
30b4(rgyud): dam tshig chags //bde ba 'bar ba'i gar byed pas//bdag la 'khyud pa'i phyag rgya mchog/dbus kyi lha mchog chen po ni //phyag rgya 'di yis rnal 'byor pa//'dod pa bzhin du 'gro
30b5(rgyud): 'ong lding//bde chen dbang phyug dpal dang ldan//rdo rje sems dpa' rabatu 'grub//mchog tu dga'-i ba rab tu sbyor ba bskal pa bzang po dpal rdo rje sems dpa'i dam tshig bde ba'i
30b6(rgyud): mchog rol pa zhes bya ba'i phyig rgya'o//se ral kha yi tshul dag tu//rdo rje kha T+wAM ga bzung ste//bdag la 'khyud ba'i phyag rgya ni//pad bzas rdo rje rgya gram mchog//phyag rgya 'di yis
30b7(rgyud): btab na ni //'dod pa bzhin du 'gre 'ong lding //dpal ldan tshe dang gzhon grub pa//rdo rje sems dpa' rab tu 'grub //mchog tu dga' ba la sbyor ba//bskal pa bzang po dpal rdo
30b8(rgyud): rje
31a1(rgyud): mnyaM sbyor @#/ O /rnal 'byor bde ba'i mchog rol pa zhes bya ba'i phyag rgya'o////rdzogs pa'i sangs rgyas kham por grub //bdag la 'khyud pa'i phyag rgya yis//rdo rje sem+sa
31a2(rgyud): dpa' dpal sbyor ba//dbus kyi lha mchog rin po'o//phyag rgya 'di yis rnal 'byor pa//'dod pa bzhin du 'gro 'ong lding //dpal ldan tshe dang gzhon grub pa//rdo rje sems dpa' rab  
31a3(rgyud): tu 'grub //mchog tu dga' ba rab tu sbyor ba bskal pa bzang po dpal bde ba'i mchog rol pa zhes bya ba'i phyag rgya'o//de la sogs pa mtha' yas mchog /chos dbyings mnyam dang mi 
31a4(rgyud): mnyam pa///namakha'i dbyings kyi mtha' klas pa'i//rang byung phyag rgya sna tshogs rnams //namakha'i kun gyi skabs na dpal //rdo rje sems dba' la gnas te//grub ba'i dbang phyug
31a5(rgyud): rab bsgrubs pas//thams cad bdag nyid 'bar bar 'gyur//sangs rgyas thams cad dang mnyam par sbyor ba//mkha'agro ma sgyu ma bde ba'i mchog gi rtsa ba'i phyag rgya'i tshogs so//gtsug
31a6(rgyud): lag ji ltar rjes mthun pa//dpal rdzogs rgya chen gar dag gis//mnga' bzhi yongs su 'phangs pa ni //bdud bzhi dag las rgyal bar 'gyur //tshangs pa chen po'i mgo dpal rdo rje 
31a7(rgyud): mda' zhes bya ba'i phyag rgya'o//rkang pa bteg cing phab pa dang//bshibs te mchongs pa'i stobs dag gis//'khor lo phyag rgyas ston na ni//khams gsum dag las mchongs par 
31b1(rgyud): 'gyur//dpal rdo rje mthu bo che zhes bya ba'i phyag rgya'o//bya ba dang ni bzlog pa dang//phrogs pa dag ni khyad par du//sna tshogs khyad par gar byed cing //khams gsum dag ni bsdu bar 
31b2(rgyud): bya//dpal rdo rje gar gyi dbang phyug ces bya ba'i phyag rgya'o//lcugs ma gang yang rung bzung nas//bdag la 'khyud pa'i phyag rgya yis//bud med thams cad brtsed na ni//dpag bsam 
31b3(rgyud): shing dag rab tu 'grub //dpal rdo rje 'dod pa'i lha mo zhes bya ba'i phyag rgya'o//myang ba brtul ba ltar byas pas//drag po dbang po nye dbang bcas//nyi zla gshin rje tshangs pa yi//'jig 
31b4(rgyud): rten gsum dag skams par byed//dpal rdo rje rta gdong zhes bya ba'i phyag rgya'o////sna'i myang ba'i rdub pa yis//drag po dbang po gnye dbang bcas//nyi zla gshani rje tshangs pa yi//
31b5(rgyud): khams gsum yang ni bdud par byed//dpal rdo rje phag mo zhes bya ba'i phyag rgya'o//mig tshugs zhen pa blta ba yis//drag po dbang po nyedabang bcas//nyi zla gshinarje tshangs pa yi//
31b6(rgyud): 'jig rten gsum yang za bar byed//dpal rdo rje spyan nyi zla me zhes bya ba'i phyag rgya'o//thams cad bdag nyid myang ba yis//drag po dbang bo nye dbang dang //nyi zla gshin rje 
31b7(rgyud): tshangs par bcas //thal bar gyur pa 'ang 'tsho bar byed //dpal rdo rje kun gyi bdag nyid khams gsum slar 'tsho ba zhes bya ba'i phyag rgya'o// //gling bu len pa'i tshul
32a1(rgyud): @#/ O /ston cing //ji ltar babs pa'i gar byed pas/tshangs pa nye dbang drag por bcas//'gro ba gsum dag dbang du byed //dpal rdo rje gling bu'i dbang zhes bya 
32a2(rgyud): ba'i phyag rgya'o//pi wang len pa'i tshul ston cing//ji ltar bcas pa'i gar byed pas//las rnams grub pa rab 'byam dang //dngos grub ma lus myur du 'grub //dpal rdo rje pi 
32a3(rgyud): wang bde bas rmongs bar byed pa zhes bya ba'i phyag rgya'o////cang te tshul ston rnal 'byor pa//ji ltar babs pa'i gar byedapas//rims dang gdon dang dug zhen pa'i//sdug bsngal 
32a4(rgyud): thams cad myur du sel //dpal rdo rje cang te'u sdug bsngal thams cad sel ba zhes bya ba'i phyag rgya'o//rdza rnga'i tshul ston rnal 'byor pa//ji ltar babs pa'i garar byed pas /gnas gsuM 
32a5(rgyud): dag ni ma lus pa//ska cig tsam gyis 'bebs par byed //dpal rdo rje rdza rnga'i myos par byed pa zhes bya ba'i phyagargya'o//phyag rgya gar gyi mchod pa yis//mchod pa thams cad byas   
32a6(rgyud): nas ni//rang byung gar gyis mchod pa rnams //ma lus lus pa med par 'grub//rims dang dug dang dbyig dug byad //phra men gdon gyi gnod pa rnams //skor ba'i gar rnam+sa
32a7(rgyud): sna tshogs kyis//skad cig tsam gyis zhi bar 'gyur //drag po dbang po nye dbang bcas//nyi zla gshin rje tshogs bcas bas//rgyas btab rma byas zos pa yang //ska cig tsam gyis 'tsho 
32b1(rgyud): bar byed//de la sogs pa'i mtha' yas mchog/chos dbyings mnyam dang mi mnyam pa //namakha'i dbyings kyi mtha' klas pa'i//rang byung lha mo'i phyag rgya rnams//namakha' kun gyi skabs na
32b2(rgyud): dpal/rdo rje sems dpa' la gnas te//dngos grub dbang phyug rab grub pas//thams cad bdag nyid 'babs par 'gyur//sangs rgyas thams cad mnyam sbyor ba//mkha' 'gro sgyu ma bde
32b3(rgyud): mchog gi//rab tu 'grub pa'i glu rnams kyis//bla na med pa'i dngos grub thob//de nas phyag rgya'i ye shes 'di//dpal ldan rdo rje mchog gi glu//las rnams thams cad rab bsgrub cing//
32b4(rgyud): srid pa shin tu bsgrub pa ni//rdo rje sems dpa' bde chen skal bzang dpal tshogs bdag las bsgrubs la rtogs par mdzad du gsol //kye de bzhin gshegs pa kun gyi bde ba mkha'
32b5(rgyud): 'gro sgyu ma sna tshogs bde ba'i mchog gis ni//'gro ba'i rgyud rnams rnam pa sna tshogs 'dul mchog 'grub pa bdag la grub par mdzad du gsol//a la la ho//de bzhin gshegs
32b6(rgyud): pa'i sna tshogs gar rnams bstan du gsol//dza hUM baM ho//kye bde ba mchog gi bde bas zung cig rnam par sprul pa hUM gis snang bar mdzad du gsol//a la la ho//de bzhin
32b7(rgyud): gshegs kun sna tshogs mkha' 'gro sgyu mas slob ma bzung du gsol//sangs rgyas thams cad mnyam sbyor ba//mkha' 'gro sgyu ma bde ba'i mchog /kun tu kun nas thams cad ni//
33a1(rgyud): mnyam sbyor @#/ O /'di nyid kyis ni myur du 'grub //sangs rgyas thams cad dang mnyam par sbyor ba mkha' 'gro sgyu ma'i rnal 'byor gyi phyag rgya shes pa'o//rnal 'byor de nyid bcom ldan 'das//
33a2(rgyud): //rdo rje sems dpa' de bzhin gshegs//thar pa thams cad rnam dag phyir//sangs rgyas phyag rgya dpal gyi gnas//don dam rnam par dag pa'i phyir//gtso ma gser gyi kha dog bzhin //chag+sa
33a3(rgyud): med la sogs chags chen phyir//dkar zhing sna tshogs gsal ba'i mdangs//chos kyi rgyal srid rab bsgrub pa//rin chen chos las lhag pa med//de bas theg chen byang chub  
33a4(rgyud): sems //rgyal ba rdo rje rin chen can//g.yas pa'i mthe bo gung mo gnyis//g.yon pa'i khu tshur bcings pa yis//so sor kun la reg pa ni//rtsa ba'i phyag rgya yin par grags//
33a5(rgyud): 'di ni sangs rgyas thams cad kyi//shes rab pha rol phyin gsal yin//byang chub sems ni bskyed pa dang//chos kyi 'khor lo bskor ba ste//phyag rgya 'di yis btab na ni//
33a6(rgyud): shes rab pha rol phyin pa'i tshul//namakha'i dbyings kyi mtha' klas par//rab tu grags par 'gyur ba yin//byang chub sems nibaskyed pa dang //chos kyi 'khor lo bskor
33a7(rgyud): ba ste//sangs rgyas nyid du rig par bya//mchog tu rtag pa'i sangs rgyas so//gang gis ldan pa 'di mthong zhing//rnam pa kun tu mchod byed pa//de yis sangs rgyas rtag pa'i mchog/
33b1(rgyud): mthong zhing mchod pa byed par 'gyur//sems ni bskyed ma thag tu yang //chos kyi 'khor lo legs par bskor//dpal dam tshig ces bya ba'i phyag rgya'o//g.yas pa'i yan lag   
33b2(rgyud): gnon pa yis//g.yon pa'i yan lag kun las rgyal//sems dpa'i dkyil mo dkrung bcas nas//bdud rnams thams cad rab tu 'joms//dpal rdo rje dkyil mo 
33b3(rgyud): dkrung ngo //rdo rje khu tshur bcas nas su//lag pa g.yon la bkab na ni//sangs rgyas kun blo dngos grub rnams //ma lus lus pa med par 'grub //de bzhin dpal ldan he ru 
33b4(rgyud): ka'i//rdo rje khu tshur che dga' ba //dpa' bo glad pa'i dngos grub rnams //ma lus lus pa med par 'grub//pad mo'i bud med khu tshur ni//de bzhin g.yon pas bkab na  
33b5(rgyud): yang //byang chub spyod pa rab 'grub rnams //ma lus lusapa med par 'grub//rin chen 'dzin pa'i khu tshur yang//lag pa g.yon pa bkab na ni//sangs rgyasanor gyi dngos 
33b6(rgyud): grub rnams//ma lus lus pa med par 'grub //sangs rgyas khu tshur bcas nas su//lag pa g.yon pas bkab na ni//sangs rgyas kun gyi tshe 'grub pa//ma lus lus pa 
33b7(rgyud): med par 'grub //bud med phyag rgya bcings nas su//lag pa g.yonapa bkab na ni//dngos grub thams cad myur 'grub pa//ma lus lus(pa) me(da) par 'grub //rta mchog dpal gyi 
34a1(rgyud): mnyaMsbyor @#/ O /khu tshur kyang //lag pa g.yon pas bkab na ni//thams cad 'gugs par rab 'grub pa //ma lus lus pa med par 'grub //dpal grub spyan gyi khu /
34a2(rgyud): tshur yang/lag pa g.yon pas bkaban ni/snang ba thams cad rab grub pa//ma lus lus pa med bar 'grub //dang po sangs rgyas kun slob pa//gnyis pa de bzhin dpa' bo 
34a3(rgyud): yin//gsum pa kun tu bzang mo'o////bzhi pa mi zad snod yin no//sangs rgyas yang dag kun dpal ldan //de bzhin zhal bzangs gzungs ma yin//sprin chen mo ni bdun 
34a4(rgyud): pa ste//brgyad pa namakha' spyan yin no//gzungs thams cad kyi phyag brgya'o//phyag rgya shes pa 'di dag gi//sangs rgyas sprul pa'i glu mchog ni//'grub bar 'gyur ba med 
34a5(rgyud): pa'i mchog/las rnams thams cad rab bsgrub pa//slong bar byed pa brtan bsgrubs pa/de bzhin sna tshogs rgyas pa dag/sha rdzi' dbyings kyi glu blangs nas//d+he ba ta yi 
34a6(rgyud): glu blangs so//'jig rten khams mang rdul phran grangs snyed khyab par 'phros pa yis//rgyal ba'i don rnams rab tu phra ba'i rdul gyi grangs snyed ba//sprul pa'i sku rnams rab 
34a7(rgyud): tu phra ba'i rdul gyi grangs snyid ni//srid pa bde chen de bzhin gshegs kun rnam 'phrul sna tshogs kyis //rdul phran grangs snyed de bzhin gshegs pa'i rnam par sprul pa mdzod//
34a8(rgyud): ho dpal rnam par snang mdzad sbyor ba la//a la la ho//de ring grub par mdzod//glu 'di dag ni blangs pa yis//nam mkha' thams cad mnyam sbyor ba//mkha' gro sgyu 
34b1(rgyud): ma gdon mi za//des na bud med bzhi rnams dang //rang gi yang dag gsang ba yis//cho ga rgyas pa'i tshul bzhin du//me tog la sogs mchod par bya//de bzhin gshegs 
34b2(rgyud): pa'i phyag rgya shes pa'o//rnal 'byor de nyid bcom ldan 'das//rdo rje sems dpa' de bzhin gshegs//dam tshig la sdang rnam sbyang phyir//dur khrod dpal gyi phyag rgyar 
34b3(rgyud): gnas//shin tu gdug cing gtum pa la//zhi bas phan par mi 'gyur te//shes rab thabs kyi rnal 'byor gyi//rnal 'byor bde mchog dam par 'gyur//
34b4(rgyud): sangs rgyas khros byung khro bo ni//thal bar 'jig pa mchog yin te//sems dpa' sngon po 'bar ba can//'bar ba'i dkyil 'khor dmar por snang //gzhan gyis 
34b5(rgyud): ma yin mi phrogs pa//lha rdzas chu yis dag pa ste//de bzhin rang bzhin nyid dag dang //mthu dang ngo bo nyid kyis 'grub//gos chen po dang klubs pa che//sha chen po dang sbrang rtsi che//
34b6(rgyud): dur khrod kun gyi 'byung po yi//phyag rgya dbang ldan dbang phyug mchog/shin tu khro gdug gtum po yi//sems can khams mang rnam sbyang phyir//'di ni dpal ldan 
34b7(rgyud): he ru ka'i//rdo rje rgya chen dbang phyug mchog/thod pa'i phreng ba'i dbu rgyan can//sangs rgyas gtsug gi nor bu dag/rgyal srid thams cad rab 'grub pa//ma lus lus 
34b8(rgyud): pa med par 'grub//rdo rje kha T+wAM rdo rjes bcas//dril bus dkrol ba'i sgra bcas pa//dpal ldan he ru ka babs pa'i//phyag rgya sna tshogs rnam 'phrul bar//
35a1(rgyud): mnyaM sbyor @#/ O /sangs rgyas rdo rje pad mo 'dzin//rdo rje nyi ma khams gsum pa//rgyas btab rmongs byas de zos nas //thal bar gyur na 'ang 'tsho bar byed //dpal 
35a2(rgyud): ldan rdo rje he ru ka rdo rje phab pa rnam par 'phrul pa pad ma gar gyi dbang phyug zhes bya ba'i phyag rgya'o//se ral kha yi tshul dag tu //kha T+wAM kha la rgyab rten byas //lag pa 
35a3(rgyud): g.yon pa thod pa thogs //g.yas pa ba dan gsum par bya//nam mkha'i phyag rgya'i rnal 'byor pa//nam mkha' kun tu 'gro bar bya //bud med la sogs rin 
35a4(rgyud): chen mchog/dngos po thams cad mkha' las 'byin//phyag rgya 'di yis yongs btab nas//bde bar lhags pa'i tshul ston pas//namakha' la ni kun blags te//
35a5(rgyud): bdag gi spyad pa de spyad do//drag po dbang po nye dbang bcas//nyi zla mkha'agro mi bzad bcas//mkha' las sgyu ma kun blangs nas//lus pa med par de 
35a6(rgyud): za'o//nam mkha' thams cad mnyam par sbyor ba bskal pa bzang po dpal rdo rje mkha' 'gro ma'i mchog ces bya ba'i phyag rgya'o////g.yas dang g.yon pa'i sbyor ba 
35a7(rgyud): yis//bde bar 'bar ba'i gar byed pas//dkyil mo dkrung ni phyed bcas la//rkang pa rang gi brlas mnan na //drag po dbang po nye dang bcas //nyi zla 
35a8(rgyud): gang shin rje tshangs bcas pa//rgyas btab rmongs byas bslangs nas su//gar dag byed cing des za'o//de nyid kyis ni sbyor ba yis//de nas bde bar 
35b1(rgyud): brla bsdams pas//legs par bsdams te phyir btags nas//rab tu bkram te btang bar bya//sgyu ma de dag nyid kyis ni//de dag brla brag bskyed

35b3(rgyud): gnas pa zhes bya ba'i phyag rgya'o//de nas gzhan yang rab bshad pa//sangs rgyas mkha' yi dkyil 'khor du//dpal ldan he ru 'bar ba yi//phyag rgya sna tshogs

35b7(rgyud): ba bde ba sbyin//mngon par gtong zhing se gol rdob//dal bus gyen du dbugs 'byin pas//g.yon du lta zhing lag pas drang //'o byed 'khyud pa de bzhin te//gar byed
35b8(rgyud): rab tu rmongs pa dang //zas dang gsang ba'i lus rnams dang //tshangs pa chen po'i mgo dang ni//de bzhin 'jig rten gsum las rgyal//de bzhin pa las 'dren pa dang/
36a1(rgyud): mnyam sbyor rang gi phyag rgya'i tshul ston dang //dur khrod dag gi gar byed dang //de bzhin rlung gi dkyil 'khor dang //rab tu 'jigs pa sreg pa'i me//

36a3(rgyud): lnga pa gar byed ma'i rgya//drug pa gsod ma'i yin par bshad//bdun pa za ba mo'i rgya//brgyad pa las kyi phyag rgya yin//de nas phyag rgya 'di kun gyi//phyag rgya'i las

39a1(rgyud): @#/ O /dang g.yon pa'i sbyor pa yis//brkyang bskum pa yi tshul byas nas//khro bo'i dkyil mo dkrung bcas te//'jig rten thams cad rab tu bzhig/

293a3(rgyud): sku la gsol mdzad ma//r+k+ta'i thig les mtshan pa mo//zhag gi za rim bdud 'joms gzugs//gdug pa thams cad 'dul mdzad ma//drag po'i zhal rnams brngams pa'i tshe//las bdag 
293a4(rgyud): chen mo'i sku ru snang //ha ha hi hi rab tu bzhad//de yi bskul ba zhag bdun bar//yang ni las la bskul bar shis//b+ha ri g+ha sma ra wa'i dza dza//zhes pa'i drag sngags bcu gcig pa//
293a5(rgyud): 'khor lo bcu gcig rtsibs ldan pa//dza dza'i 'og tu ming yang sbe//bdun 'bum sngags ni tshang bar bzlas//u rgyan ngos su bskyal bar bya//des ni the tshom med par 'gro//
293a6(rgyud): phag mo mngon par 'byung ba'i rgyud las//gnas kyi mtshan nyid dang bcas pa'i zhal gcig ma'i las bstan pa'i le'u ste//bcu drug pa'o////sems dang 
293a7(rgyud): rjes su mthun pa'i rnal 'byor ma//mnyam par bzhag pa'i sems bskam na//shes rab bsod nams dman pas yang //'grub 'gyur 'di la the tshom med//bla ma sangs rgyas bla ma 
293b1(rgyud): chos//de phyir bla ma dge 'dun te//dpal ldan bla ma rdo rje 'chang //skabs 'dir bla mas byed pa'o//'bras bu sangs rgyas 'dod pa yis//bla ma de bzhin bsnyen bkur bya//rab tu 
293b2(rgyud): spags pa'i dbu rgyan nas byung ba'i rgyud ba/bla ma med pa'i dgongs pa 'di dpal rdo rje rnal 'byor ma'i gsang chen/phag mo mngon par 'byung ba'i rgyud kyi rgyal po rdzogas+ho//
293b3(rgyud): ////rje btsun 'brog mi shAkya ye shes kyi zhal snga nas dang /lots+tsha ba gyi jo zla ba'i 'od zer gyis bsgyur ba'o//
293b4(rgyud): rgyud 'di la la dag the tshom can du 'dod do//pu ti 'di la sangs rgyas thams cad dang mnyam par sbyor ba mkha' 'gro ma sgyu ma bde mchog gi bsdus rgyud brtag pa dang /rgyud phyi ma'i 
293b5(rgyud): phyi ma rtog pa lnga dang /bde mchog rtsa rgyud 'gyur snga gyi gnyis ka dang /he ru ka mngon 'byung dang /rnal 'byor ma kun spyod dang /phag mo mngon par byang chub pa'i rgyud dang /
293b6(rgyud): phag mo mngon par 'byung pa'i rgyud pa ni ta dz+nyA na ka ra dang /lots+tsha ba khu sngon dngos grub kyis bsgyur ba dang /phag mo mngon phag 'byung ba rtsa ba'i rgyud phyi ma shAkya ye shes dang /lots+tsha ba 
293b7(rgyud): gyi jo zla ba'i 'od zer kyis bsgyur ba rnams gzhags pa yin no/phag mo mngon par byang cub pa'i rgyud dang /phag mo mngon/'byung ba'i rgyud snga ma 'di nyid rgya skad yod med 
293b8(rgyud): ma rtogs/lus dang 'gyur byed sogs la khyad par mi 'dug pas/'di gnyis ldabs pa yin nam/de ltar yin par mkhas pa rnams kyis brtag par gyis shig/kar chag
